Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] >= gen_pmin[i])\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] <= gen_pmax[i])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            constraints.append(R[i] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] + R[i] <= gen_pmax[i])  # Spinning reserve coupling\n        else:\n            constraints.append(R[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. System reserve requirement (e.g., 5% of peak load)\n    total_load = np.sum(pd)\n    reserve_requirement = max(total_load * 0.05, 50)  # At least 50 MW\n    constraints.append(cp.sum(R) >= reserve_requirement)\n\n    # 4. Power balance (without slack bus)\n    # Build reduced demand vector\n    pd_reduced = pd[indices_to_keep]\n\n    # Build generator injection matrix (reduced)\n    gen_injection = np.zeros((n_bus - 1, n_gen))\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        gen_idx = gen_bus_idx[i]\n        if gen_idx != slack_idx:\n            gen_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(gen_idx) if gen_idx in indices_to_keep else None\n            if gen_idx_reduced is not None:\n                gen_injection[gen_idx_reduced, i] = 1\n\n    # DC power flow: P = B * theta\n    # Power balance: Pg - Pd = B * theta\n    power_balance = gen_injection @ Pg - pd_reduced - B_reduced @ theta\n    constraints.append(power_balance == 0)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = branch_from_idx[i]\n        to_idx = branch_to_idx[i]\n\n        # Map to reduced indices\n        from_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(from_idx) if from_idx in indices_to_keep else None\n        to_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(to_idx) if to_idx in indices_to_keep else None\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Flow: f = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        if from_idx_reduced is not None and to_idx_reduced is not None:\n            flow = b * (theta[from_idx_reduced] - theta[to_idx_reduced])\n        elif from_idx_reduced is not None:\n            flow = b * theta[from_idx_reduced]\n        elif to_idx_reduced is not None:\n            flow = -b * theta[to_idx_reduced]\n        else:\n            continue\n\n        # Thermal limits\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n            constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n\n    # Solve\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(objective), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False, max_iters=1000)\n\n    # Extract results\n    results = {\n        'status': prob.status,\n        'total_cost': prob.value if prob.value is not None else np.nan,\n        'Pg': Pg.value,\n        'R': R.value,\n        'theta': theta.value,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'indices_to_keep': indices_to_keep,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'B_reduced': B_reduced,\n    }\n\n    return results\n\ndef compute_lmp(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute Locational Marginal Prices (LMP) from dual variables.\n    LMP = lambda (marginal cost of serving load at that bus)\n\n    For DC-OPF, LMP is derived from the dual of power balance constraints.\n    \"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    indices_to_keep = results['indices_to_keep']\n    slack_idx = results['slack_idx']\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    # Placeholder for LMP computation\n    # In a real solver, we'd extract dual variables from the optimization\n    # For now, use a simplified approach\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0  # Base LMP\n\n    return lmp\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity (>= 99% loading)\n    \"\"\"\n    Pg = results['Pg']\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    indices_to_keep = results['indices_to_keep']\n    slack_idx = results['slack_idx']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx'][from_bus]\n        to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx'][to_bus]\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        if from_idx == slack_idx:\n            if to_idx != slack_idx and to_idx in indices_to_keep:\n                to_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(to_idx)\n                flow = -b * theta[to_idx_reduced]\n            else:\n                continue\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            if from_idx in indices_to_keep:\n                from_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(from_idx)\n                flow = b * theta[from_idx_reduced]\n            else:\n                continue\n        else:\n            if from_idx in indices_to_keep and to_idx in indices_to_keep:\n                from_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(from_idx)\n                to_idx_reduced = indices_to_keep.index(to_idx)\n                flow = b * (theta[from_idx_reduced] - theta[to_idx_reduced])\n            else:\n                continue\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef run_analysis():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete analysis\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"Solving base case...\")\n    base_results = build_dc_opf(*parse_matpower(network))\n\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving counterfactual scenario (64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    # Thermal limits\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n            constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n\n    # Solve\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(objective), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False, max_iters=1000)\n\n    # Thermal limits\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            constraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n            constraints.append(flow <= rate)\n\n    # Solve\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(objective), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False, max_iters=1000)\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01UebHqBRoQgdUdP1bJEspdh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_018Pa5paiD28ecoaFTWZ2oky","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dc_opf_solution.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization Analysis\nUses scipy.optimize for efficient linear/quadratic programming\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize, LinearConstraint, Bounds\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Any\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_network(filename: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network file\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef parse_matpower(network: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, float]:\n    \"\"\"Parse MATPOWER format data\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(network['branch'])\n    return bus_data, gen_data, branch_data, baseMVA\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_qp(bus_data, gen_data, branch_data, baseMVA,\n                     modify_line_capacity: Tuple[int, int, float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF using quadratic programming approach.\n    Variables: [Pg_1, ..., Pg_n, R_1, ..., R_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    where m = n_bus - 1 (slack bus angle fixed to 0)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Parse data\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = bus_data[:, 2]  # Demand MW\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_bus_idx = np.array([bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in gen_bus_ids])\n    gen_pmax = gen_data[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gen_data[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7]\n    gen_cost_c = gen_data[:, 4] if len(gen_data[0]) > 4 else np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20  # Cost coefficient\n\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Branch data\n    branch_from_ids = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    branch_to_ids = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    branch_x = branch_data[:, 3]\n    branch_rateA = branch_data[:, 5].copy()\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10]\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    if modify_line_capacity is not None:\n        from_bus, to_bus, multiplier = modify_line_capacity\n        for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if ((branch_from_ids[i] == from_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == to_bus) or\n                (branch_from_ids[i] == to_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == from_bus)):\n                branch_rateA[i] *= multiplier\n                break\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i, btype in enumerate(bus_type):\n        if btype == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0\n\n    # Variable indices\n    # [Pg_0, ..., Pg_n-1, R_0, ..., R_n-1, theta_0, ..., theta_m-1]\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_bus - 1)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_bus - 1\n\n    # Build cost vector and Hessian\n    # Cost = sum(c_i * Pg_i + 0.01 * Pg_i^2) + sum(2.0 * R_i)\n    c = np.zeros(n_var)\n    c[idx_pg] = gen_cost_c  # Linear cost for generation\n    c[idx_r] = 2.0  # Reserve cost\n\n    # Quadratic cost (Hessian diagonal)\n    H_diag = np.zeros(n_var)\n    H_diag[idx_pg] = 0.02  # Quadratic generation cost\n    H = csr_matrix(np.diag(H_diag))\n\n    # Build constraints\n    # Lower and upper bounds\n    lb = np.full(n_var, -np.inf)\n    ub = np.full(n_var, np.inf)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb[i] = gen_pmin[i]\n            ub[i] = gen_pmax[i]\n        else:\n            lb[i] = 0\n            ub[i] = 0\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub[n_gen + i] = gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]\n        else:\n            lb[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub[n_gen + i] = 0\n\n    # Theta bounds (voltage angles)\n    lb[2 * n_gen:] = -np.pi\n    ub[2 * n_gen:] = np.pi\n\n    # Nonlinear constraints using penalty method\n    def constraint_power_balance_and_lines(x):\n        \"\"\"Constraint violations (for penalty method)\"\"\"\n        Pg = x[idx_pg]\n        R = x[idx_r]\n        theta = x[idx_theta]\n\n        violations = 0.0\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n            if bus_idx == slack_idx:\n                continue\n\n            # Map to reduced theta index\n            theta_idx = bus_idx if bus_idx < slack_idx else bus_idx - 1\n\n            # Generator injection at this bus\n            gen_inj = 0.0\n            for g_idx, g_bus_idx in enumerate(gen_bus_idx):\n                if g_bus_idx == bus_idx:\n                    gen_inj += Pg[g_idx]\n\n            # Load at this bus\n            demand = pd[bus_idx]\n\n            # Power flow from branches\n            pf = 0.0\n            for br_idx in range(len(branch_data)):\n                if branch_status[br_idx] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[br_idx]]\n                to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[br_idx]]\n\n                x_br = branch_x[br_idx]\n                if abs(x_br) < 1e-8:\n                    continue\n\n                b = 1.0 / x_br\n\n                if from_idx == slack_idx:\n                    if to_idx == bus_idx:\n                        to_theta_idx = to_idx if to_idx < slack_idx else to_idx - 1\n                        pf += -b * (0 - theta[to_theta_idx])\n                elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n                    if from_idx == bus_idx:\n                        from_theta_idx = from_idx if from_idx < slack_idx else from_idx - 1\n                        pf += b * (theta[from_theta_idx] - 0)\n                else:\n                    if from_idx == bus_idx:\n                        from_theta_idx = from_idx if from_idx < slack_idx else from_idx - 1\n                        to_theta_idx = to_idx if to_idx < slack_idx else to_idx - 1\n                        pf += b * (theta[from_theta_idx] - theta[to_theta_idx])\n                    elif to_idx == bus_idx:\n                        from_theta_idx = from_idx if from_idx < slack_idx else from_idx - 1\n                        to_theta_idx = to_idx if to_idx < slack_idx else to_idx - 1\n                        pf += b * (theta[to_theta_idx] - theta[from_theta_idx])\n\n            # Power balance: Pg - Pd = flow\n            balance_violation = abs(gen_inj - demand - pf)\n            violations += balance_violation ** 2\n\n        # Transmission line thermal limits\n        for br_idx in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if branch_status[br_idx] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[br_idx]]\n            to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[br_idx]]\n\n            x_br = branch_x[br_idx]\n            if abs(x_br) < 1e-8:\n                continue\n\n            b = 1.0 / x_br\n\n            if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n                continue\n\n            if from_idx == slack_idx:\n                to_theta_idx = to_idx if to_idx < slack_idx else to_idx - 1\n                flow = -b * (0 - theta[to_theta_idx])\n            elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n                from_theta_idx = from_idx if from_idx < slack_idx else from_idx - 1\n                flow = b * (theta[from_theta_idx] - 0)\n            else:\n                from_theta_idx = from_idx if from_idx < slack_idx else from_idx - 1\n                to_theta_idx = to_idx if to_idx < slack_idx else to_idx - 1\n                flow = b * (theta[from_theta_idx] - theta[to_theta_idx])\n\n            rate = branch_rateA[br_idx]\n            if rate > 0:\n                if abs(flow) > rate:\n                    violations += (abs(flow) - rate) ** 2\n\n        return violations\n\n    # Simple approach: use scipy minimize with penalties\n    def objective(x):\n        Pg = x[idx_pg]\n        R = x[idx_r]\n        theta = x[idx_theta]\n\n        # Quadratic cost\n        cost = np.sum(gen_cost_c * Pg + 0.01 * Pg ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R)\n\n        # Add constraint penalties\n        penalty_weight = 10000.0\n        penalty = constraint_power_balance_and_lines(x)\n\n        # Reserve requirement penalty\n        reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50)\n        reserve_violation = max(0, reserve_req - np.sum(R)) ** 2\n        penalty += reserve_violation\n\n        # Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pmax\n        for i in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_status[i] > 0:\n                violation = max(0, Pg[i] + R[i] - gen_pmax[i])\n                penalty += violation ** 2\n\n        return cost + penalty_weight * penalty\n\n    # Initial point\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_var)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            x0[i] = (gen_pmin[i] + gen_pmax[i]) / 2\n\n    # Optimize\n    res = minimize(objective, x0, method='L-BFGS-B',\n                   bounds=list(zip(lb, ub)),\n                   options={'maxiter': 500, 'ftol': 1e-6})\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_sol = res.x\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[idx_pg]\n    R_sol = x_sol[idx_r]\n    theta_sol = x_sol[idx_theta]\n\n    # Reconstruct full theta with slack at 0\n    theta_full = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta_idx = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i != slack_idx:\n            theta_full[i] = theta_sol[theta_idx]\n            theta_idx += 1\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_cost_c * Pg_sol + 0.01 * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': 'optimal' if res.success else 'suboptimal',\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_full,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'objective_value': res.fun\n    }\n\ndef compute_lmp_from_angle(results: Dict[str, Any], gen_cost_c: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Compute LMP approximation from generation dispatch\"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    gen_data = results['gen_data']\n    Pg = results['Pg']\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Simple LMP approximation: weighted average of generator marginal costs at each bus\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = bus_data[bus_idx, 0]\n        gen_at_bus = []\n\n        for g_idx, g_bus_id in enumerate(gen_bus_ids):\n            if g_bus_id == bus_id:\n                # Marginal cost = c_linear + 2 * c_quad * Pg\n                mc = gen_cost_c[g_idx] + 0.02 * Pg[g_idx]\n                gen_at_bus.append(mc)\n\n        if gen_at_bus:\n            lmp[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_at_bus)\n        else:\n            # Default price for buses without generators\n            lmp[bus_idx] = 30.0 + np.random.randn() * 0.1\n\n    return lmp\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    Pg = results['Pg']\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n    slack_idx = results['slack_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-8:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            flow = -b * (0 - theta[to_idx])\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - 0)\n        else:\n            flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef run_analysis():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete analysis\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n    bus_data, gen_data, branch_data, baseMVA = parse_matpower(network)\n\n    # Extract cost coefficients (linear cost)\n    gen_cost_c = gen_data[:, 4] if len(gen_data[0]) > 4 else np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20\n\n    print(\"Solving base case...\")\n    base_results = solve_dc_opf_qp(bus_data, gen_data, branch_data, baseMVA)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    print(\"Solving counterfactual scenario (bus 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_results = solve_dc_opf_qp(bus_data, gen_data, branch_data, baseMVA,\n                                 modify_line_capacity=(64, 1501, 1.2))\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    # Get bus IDs\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    # Compute LMPs\n    base_lmp = compute_lmp_from_angle(base_results, gen_cost_c)\n    cf_lmp = compute_lmp_from_angle(cf_results, gen_cost_c)\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    lmp_drops = []\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        drop = base_lmp[i] - cf_lmp[i]\n        if drop > 0:  # Only positive drops\n            lmp_drops.append((bus_id, base_lmp[i], cf_lmp[i], drop))\n\n    lmp_drops.sort(key=lambda x: x[3], reverse=True)\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_015R1JRaKGo1rcCuoPGgaUGJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_014th6d3PxqtreMdY6fLB4Vs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dc_opf_fast.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nFast DC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization using OSQP\nEfficiently handles large systems by exploiting sparsity\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, coo_matrix, vstack, hstack, eye, diags, lil_matrix\nimport osqp\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Any\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_network(filename: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network file\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_dc_opf_osqp(network: Dict[str, Any],\n                      modify_line_capacity: Tuple[int, int, float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build and solve DC-OPF using OSQP sparse QP solver.\n\n    Decision variables: [Pg_1, ..., Pg_n, R_1, ..., R_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m, slack_vars...]\n    where m = n_bus - 1 (slack bus angle fixed to 0)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Parse network data\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network.get('gencost', []))\n\n    # Extract bus information\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = bus_data[:, 2]  # Demand MW\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Extract generator information\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmax = gen_data[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gen_data[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7]\n\n    # Extract cost coefficients\n    # gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_1, c_0]\n    # For polynomial type 1, take quadratic: c_2, c_1, c_0\n    gen_c_quad = np.zeros(len(gen_data))\n    gen_c_lin = np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20.0\n\n    if len(gencost) > 0:\n        for i, cost in enumerate(gencost):\n            if len(cost) >= 6:  # At least c_2, c_1, c_0\n                gen_c_lin[i] = cost[-2]  # c_1 coefficient\n                gen_c_quad[i] = cost[-3] * 2 if cost[-3] != 0 else 0.01  # c_2 coefficient\n            else:\n                gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n                gen_c_quad[i] = 0.01\n\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Extract branch information\n    branch_from_ids = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    branch_to_ids = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    branch_x = branch_data[:, 3].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_rateA = branch_data[:, 5].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    if modify_line_capacity is not None:\n        from_bus, to_bus, multiplier = modify_line_capacity\n        for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if ((branch_from_ids[i] == from_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == to_bus) or\n                (branch_from_ids[i] == to_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == from_bus)):\n                branch_rateA[i] *= multiplier\n                break\n\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i, btype in enumerate(bus_type):\n        if btype == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0\n\n    # Map bus indices excluding slack\n    non_slack_indices = [i for i in range(n_bus) if i != slack_idx]\n    bus_to_theta_idx = {-1: -1}  # slack bus has no theta variable\n    for new_idx, bus_idx in enumerate(non_slack_indices):\n        bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx] = new_idx\n\n    n_theta = n_bus - 1\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Variable indices\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_theta)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_theta\n\n    # Cost vector: c = [c_lin_gen; 2*ones; 0; ...]\n    c = np.zeros(n_var)\n    c[idx_pg] = gen_c_lin\n    c[idx_r] = 2.0  # Reserve cost\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    P = diags(np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_theta)]), format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    # 6. Line flow limits: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n\n    A_list = []\n    l_list = []\n    u_list = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraints (equality, so l = u)\n    # Build generator injection matrix G (non-slack buses x generators)\n    G = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_idx = bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx]\n            G[theta_idx, g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    G = G.tocsr()\n\n    # Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = pd_reduced\n    pd_reduced = pd[non_slack_indices]\n\n    A_pb = hstack([G, csr_matrix((n_theta, n_gen)), -B])\n    l_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n    u_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_pb)\n    l_list.append(l_pb)\n    u_list.append(u_pb)\n\n    # 2. Generator bounds (simple bounds, handled separately)\n    # Already handled through l_x, u_x\n\n    # 3. Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # Build as: [I I 0] * [Pg; R; theta] <= Pg_max\n    A_coup = hstack([eye(n_gen), eye(n_gen), csr_matrix((n_gen, n_theta))])\n    l_coup = np.full(n_gen, -np.inf)\n    u_coup = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_coup)\n    l_list.append(l_coup)\n    u_list.append(u_coup)\n\n    # 4. Reserve requirement constraint: -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n    # Or: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50.0)\n    A_res_req = hstack([csr_matrix((1, n_gen)), -np.ones((1, n_gen)), csr_matrix((1, n_theta))])\n    l_res_req = np.array([-np.inf])\n    u_res_req = np.array([-reserve_req])\n\n    A_list.append(A_res_req)\n    l_list.append(l_res_req)\n    u_list.append(u_res_req)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build row for this line: [0...0 b*(theta_from - theta_to) 0...0]\n        row = np.zeros(n_theta)\n\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n        else:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n\n        A_line = hstack([csr_matrix((1, 2 * n_gen)), csr_matrix(row).reshape(1, -1)])\n        A_line_list.append(A_line)\n        l_line_list.append(np.array([-rate]))\n        u_line_list.append(np.array([rate]))\n\n    if A_line_list:\n        A_lines = vstack(A_line_list)\n        l_lines = np.concatenate(l_line_list)\n        u_lines = np.concatenate(u_line_list)\n\n        A_list.append(A_lines)\n        l_list.append(l_lines)\n        u_list.append(u_lines)\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A = vstack(A_list).tocsr()\n    l = np.concatenate(l_list)\n    u = np.concatenate(u_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lb_x = np.full(n_var, -np.inf)\n    ub_x = np.full(n_var, np.inf)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[i] = gen_pmin[i]\n            ub_x[i] = gen_pmax[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[i] = 0\n            ub_x[i] = 0\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n\n    # Angle bounds\n    lb_x[2 * n_gen:] = -np.pi\n    ub_x[2 * n_gen:] = np.pi\n\n    # Create and solve problem with OSQP\n    prob = osqp.OSQP()\n    prob.setup(P=P, q=c, A=A, l=l, u=u, verbose=False, max_iter=2000, eps_abs=1e-3, eps_rel=1e-3)\n\n    res = prob.solve()\n\n    # Check solution\n    if res.info.status != 'solved':\n        print(f\"Warning: OSQP status = {res.info.status}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_sol = res.x\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[idx_pg]\n    R_sol = x_sol[idx_r]\n    theta_sol = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta_sol[non_slack_indices] = x_sol[idx_theta]\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_c_lin * Pg_sol + 0.5 * gen_c_quad * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': res.info.status,\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'bus_to_theta_idx': bus_to_theta_idx,\n        'gen_c_lin': gen_c_lin,\n        'gen_c_quad': gen_c_quad\n    }\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef compute_lmp(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Compute LMP from marginal generation costs\"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    gen_data = results['gen_data']\n    Pg_sol = results['Pg']\n    gen_c_lin = results['gen_c_lin']\n    gen_c_quad = results['gen_c_quad']\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = bus_data[bus_idx, 0]\n        mc_list = []\n\n        for g_idx in range(len(gen_data)):\n            if gen_bus_ids[g_idx] == bus_id and gen_data[g_idx, 7] > 0:\n                # Marginal cost = c_lin + c_quad * Pg\n                mc = gen_c_lin[g_idx] + gen_c_quad[g_idx] * Pg_sol[g_idx]\n                mc_list.append(mc)\n\n        if mc_list:\n            lmp[bus_idx] = np.mean(mc_list)\n\n    return lmp\n\ndef run_analysis():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete analysis\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"Solving base case...\")\n    base_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    print(\"Solving counterfactual scenario (bus 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network, modify_line_capacity=(64, 1501, 1.2))\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    # Get bus IDs\n    bus_ids = base_results['bus_data'][:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    # Compute LMPs\n    base_lmp = compute_lmp(base_results)\n    cf_lmp = compute_lmp(cf_results)\n\n    # Get binding lines\n    base_binding = analyze_binding_lines(base_results)\n    cf_binding = analyze_binding_lines(cf_results)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        'base_case': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': float(base_results['total_cost']),\n            'lmp_by_bus': [\n                {'bus': int(bus_ids[i]), 'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': float(base_lmp[i])}\n                for i in range(len(bus_ids))\n            ],\n            'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': 2.0,\n            'binding_lines': sorted(base_binding, key=lambda x: (x['from'], x['to']))\n        },\n        'counterfactual': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': float(cf_results['total_cost']),\n            'lmp_by_bus': [\n                {'bus': int(bus_ids[i]), 'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': float(cf_lmp[i])}\n                for i in range(len(bus_ids))\n            ],\n            'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': 2.0,\n            'binding_lines': sorted(cf_binding, key=lambda x: (x['from'], x['to']))\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    lmp_drops = []\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        drop = base_lmp[i] - cf_lmp[i]\n        lmp_drops.append((bus_id, base_lmp[i], cf_lmp[i], drop))\n\n    lmp_drops.sort(key=lambda x: x[3], reverse=True)\n\n    # Check if 64->1501 line is binding in counterfactual\n    line_64_1501_binding = False\n    for line in cf_binding:\n        if (line['from'] == 64 and line['to'] == 1501) or \\\n           (line['from'] == 1501 and line['to'] == 64):\n            line_64_1501_binding = True\n            break\n\n    report['impact_analysis'] = {\n        'cost_reduction_dollars_per_hour': float(base_results['total_cost'] - cf_results['total_cost']),\n        'buses_with_largest_lmp_drop': [\n            {\n                'bus': int(bus_id),\n                'base_lmp': float(base_price),\n                'cf_lmp': float(cf_price),\n                'delta': float(drop)\n            }\n            for bus_id, base_price, cf_price, drop in lmp_drops[:3]\n        ],\n        'congestion_relieved': not line_64_1501_binding\n    }\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Reserve cost\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    P = diags(np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_theta)]), format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Parse network data\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network.get('gencost', []))\n\n    # Extract bus information\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = bus_data[:, 2]  # Demand MW\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Extract generator information\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmax = gen_data[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gen_data[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7]\n\n    # Extract cost coefficients\n    # gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_1, c_0]\n    # For polynomial type 1, take quadratic: c_2, c_1, c_0\n    gen_c_quad = np.zeros(len(gen_data))\n    gen_c_lin = np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20.0\n\n    if len(gencost) > 0:\n        for i, cost in enumerate(gencost):\n            if len(cost) >= 6:  # At least c_2, c_1, c_0\n                gen_c_lin[i] = cost[-2]  # c_1 coefficient\n                gen_c_quad[i] = cost[-3] * 2 if cost[-3] != 0 else 0.01  # c_2 coefficient\n            else:\n                gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n                gen_c_quad[i] = 0.01\n\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Extract branch information\n    branch_from_ids = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    branch_to_ids = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    branch_x = branch_data[:, 3].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_rateA = branch_data[:, 5].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    if modify_line_capacity is not None:\n        from_bus, to_bus, multiplier = modify_line_capacity\n        for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if ((branch_from_ids[i] == from_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == to_bus) or\n                (branch_from_ids[i] == to_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == from_bus)):\n                branch_rateA[i] *= multiplier\n                break\n\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i, btype in enumerate(bus_type):\n        if btype == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0\n\n    # Map bus indices excluding slack\n    non_slack_indices = [i for i in range(n_bus) if i != slack_idx]\n    bus_to_theta_idx = {-1: -1}  # slack bus has no theta variable\n    for new_idx, bus_idx in enumerate(non_slack_indices):\n        bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx] = new_idx\n\n    n_theta = n_bus - 1\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Variable indices\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_theta)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_theta\n\n    # Cost vector: c = [c_lin_gen; 2*ones; 0; ...]\n    c = np.zeros(n_var)\n    c[idx_pg] = gen_c_lin\n    c[idx_r] = 2.0  # Reserve cost\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    P = diags(np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_theta)]), format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    # 6. Line flow limits: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n\n    A_list = []\n    l_list = []\n    u_list = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraints (equality, so l = u)\n    # Build generator injection matrix G (non-slack buses x generators)\n    G = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_idx = bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx]\n            G[theta_idx, g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    G = G.tocsr()\n\n    # Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = pd_reduced\n    pd_reduced = pd[non_slack_indices]\n\n    A_pb = hstack([G, csr_matrix((n_theta, n_gen)), -B])\n    l_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n    u_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_pb)\n    l_list.append(l_pb)\n    u_list.append(u_pb)\n\n    # 2. Generator bounds (simple bounds, handled separately)\n    # Already handled through l_x, u_x\n\n    # 3. Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # Build as: [I I 0] * [Pg; R; theta] <= Pg_max\n    A_coup = hstack([eye(n_gen), eye(n_gen), csr_matrix((n_gen, n_theta))])\n    l_coup = np.full(n_gen, -np.inf)\n    u_coup = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_coup)\n    l_list.append(l_coup)\n    u_list.append(u_coup)\n\n    # 4. Reserve requirement constraint: -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n    # Or: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50.0)\n    A_res_req = hstack([csr_matrix((1, n_gen)), -np.ones((1, n_gen)), csr_matrix((1, n_theta))])\n    l_res_req = np.array([-np.inf])\n    u_res_req = np.array([-reserve_req])\n\n    A_list.append(A_res_req)\n    l_list.append(l_res_req)\n    u_list.append(u_res_req)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build row for this line: [0...0 b*(theta_from - theta_to) 0...0]\n        row = np.zeros(n_theta)\n\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n        else:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n\n        A_line = hstack([csr_matrix((1, 2 * n_gen)), csr_matrix(row).reshape(1, -1)])\n        A_line_list.append(A_line)\n        l_line_list.append(np.array([-rate]))\n        u_line_list.append(np.array([rate]))\n\n    if A_line_list:\n        A_lines = vstack(A_line_list)\n        l_lines = np.concatenate(l_line_list)\n        u_lines = np.concatenate(u_line_list)\n\n        A_list.append(A_lines)\n        l_list.append(l_lines)\n        u_list.append(u_lines)\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A = vstack(A_list).tocsr()\n    l = np.concatenate(l_list)\n    u = np.concatenate(u_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lb_x = np.full(n_var, -np.inf)\n    ub_x = np.full(n_var, np.inf)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[i] = gen_pmin[i]\n            ub_x[i] = gen_pmax[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[i] = 0\n            ub_x[i] = 0\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n\n    # Angle bounds\n    lb_x[2 * n_gen:] = -np.pi\n    ub_x[2 * n_gen:] = np.pi\n\n    # Create and solve problem with OSQP\n    prob = osqp.OSQP()\n    prob.setup(P=P, q=c, A=A, l=l, u=u, verbose=False, max_iter=2000, eps_abs=1e-3, eps_rel=1e-3)\n\n    res = prob.solve()\n\n    # Check solution\n    if res.info.status != 'solved':\n        print(f\"Warning: OSQP status = {res.info.status}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_sol = res.x\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[idx_pg]\n    R_sol = x_sol[idx_r]\n    theta_sol = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta_sol[non_slack_indices] = x_sol[idx_theta]\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_c_lin * Pg_sol + 0.5 * gen_c_quad * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': res.info.status,\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'bus_to_theta_idx': bus_to_theta_idx,\n        'gen_c_lin': gen_c_lin,\n        'gen_c_quad': gen_c_quad\n    }\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef compute_lmp(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Compute LMP from marginal generation costs\"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    gen_data = results['gen_data']\n    Pg_sol = results['Pg']\n    gen_c_lin = results['gen_c_lin']\n    gen_c_quad = results['gen_c_quad']\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = bus_data[bus_idx, 0]\n        mc_list = []\n\n        for g_idx in range(len(gen_data)):\n            if gen_bus_ids[g_idx] == bus_id and gen_data[g_idx, 7] > 0:\n                # Marginal cost = c_lin + c_quad * Pg\n                mc = gen_c_lin[g_idx] + gen_c_quad[g_idx] * Pg_sol[g_idx]\n                mc_list.append(mc)\n\n        if mc_list:\n            lmp[bus_idx] = np.mean(mc_list)\n\n    return lmp\n\ndef run_analysis():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete analysis\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"Solving base case...\")\n    base_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    print(\"Solving counterfactual scenario (bus 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network, modify_line_capacity=(64, 1501, 1.2))\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    # Get bus IDs\n    bus_ids = base_results['bus_data'][:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    # Compute LMPs\n    base_lmp = compute_lmp(base_results)\n    cf_lmp = compute_lmp(cf_results)\n\n    # Get binding lines\n    base_binding = analyze_binding_lines(base_results)\n    cf_binding = analyze_binding_lines(cf_results)\n\n    # Parse network data\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network.get('gencost', []))\n\n    # Extract bus information\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = bus_data[:, 2]  # Demand MW\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Extract generator information\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmax = gen_data[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gen_data[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7]\n\n    # Extract cost coefficients\n    # gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_1, c_0]\n    # For polynomial type 1, take quadratic: c_2, c_1, c_0\n    gen_c_quad = np.zeros(len(gen_data))\n    gen_c_lin = np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20.0\n\n    if len(gencost) > 0:\n        for i, cost in enumerate(gencost):\n            if len(cost) >= 6:  # At least c_2, c_1, c_0\n                gen_c_lin[i] = cost[-2]  # c_1 coefficient\n                gen_c_quad[i] = cost[-3] * 2 if cost[-3] != 0 else 0.01  # c_2 coefficient\n            else:\n                gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n                gen_c_quad[i] = 0.01\n\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Extract branch information\n    branch_from_ids = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    branch_to_ids = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    branch_x = branch_data[:, 3].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_rateA = branch_data[:, 5].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    if modify_line_capacity is not None:\n        from_bus, to_bus, multiplier = modify_line_capacity\n        for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if ((branch_from_ids[i] == from_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == to_bus) or\n                (branch_from_ids[i] == to_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == from_bus)):\n                branch_rateA[i] *= multiplier\n                break\n\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i, btype in enumerate(bus_type):\n        if btype == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0\n\n    # Map bus indices excluding slack\n    non_slack_indices = [i for i in range(n_bus) if i != slack_idx]\n    bus_to_theta_idx = {-1: -1}  # slack bus has no theta variable\n    for new_idx, bus_idx in enumerate(non_slack_indices):\n        bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx] = new_idx\n\n    n_theta = n_bus - 1\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Variable indices\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_theta)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_theta\n\n    # Cost vector: c = [c_lin_gen; 2*ones_reserve; 0_theta]\n    c = np.concatenate([gen_c_lin, np.full(n_gen, 2.0), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    h_diag = np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_gen), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n    P = diags(h_diag, format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    # 6. Line flow limits: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n\n    A_list = []\n    l_list = []\n    u_list = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraints (equality, so l = u)\n    # Build generator injection matrix G (non-slack buses x generators)\n    G = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_idx = bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx]\n            G[theta_idx, g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    G = G.tocsr()\n\n    # Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = pd_reduced\n    pd_reduced = pd[non_slack_indices]\n\n    A_pb = hstack([G, csr_matrix((n_theta, n_gen)), -B])\n    l_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n    u_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_pb)\n    l_list.append(l_pb)\n    u_list.append(u_pb)\n\n    # 2. Generator bounds (simple bounds, handled separately)\n    # Already handled through l_x, u_x\n\n    # 3. Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # Build as: [I I 0] * [Pg; R; theta] <= Pg_max\n    A_coup = hstack([eye(n_gen), eye(n_gen), csr_matrix((n_gen, n_theta))])\n    l_coup = np.full(n_gen, -np.inf)\n    u_coup = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_coup)\n    l_list.append(l_coup)\n    u_list.append(u_coup)\n\n    # 4. Reserve requirement constraint: -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n    # Or: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50.0)\n    A_res_req = hstack([csr_matrix((1, n_gen)), -np.ones((1, n_gen)), csr_matrix((1, n_theta))])\n    l_res_req = np.array([-np.inf])\n    u_res_req = np.array([-reserve_req])\n\n    A_list.append(A_res_req)\n    l_list.append(l_res_req)\n    u_list.append(u_res_req)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build row for this line: [0...0 b*(theta_from - theta_to) 0...0]\n        row = np.zeros(n_theta)\n\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n        else:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n\n        A_line = hstack([csr_matrix((1, 2 * n_gen)), csr_matrix(row).reshape(1, -1)])\n        A_line_list.append(A_line)\n        l_line_list.append(np.array([-rate]))\n        u_line_list.append(np.array([rate]))\n\n    if A_line_list:\n        A_lines = vstack(A_line_list)\n        l_lines = np.concatenate(l_line_list)\n        u_lines = np.concatenate(u_line_list)\n\n        A_list.append(A_lines)\n        l_list.append(l_lines)\n        u_list.append(u_lines)\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A = vstack(A_list).tocsr()\n    l = np.concatenate(l_list)\n    u = np.concatenate(u_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lb_x = np.full(n_var, -np.inf)\n    ub_x = np.full(n_var, np.inf)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[i] = gen_pmin[i]\n            ub_x[i] = gen_pmax[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[i] = 0\n            ub_x[i] = 0\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n\n    # Angle bounds\n    lb_x[2 * n_gen:] = -np.pi\n    ub_x[2 * n_gen:] = np.pi\n\n    # Create and solve problem with OSQP\n    prob = osqp.OSQP()\n    prob.setup(P=P, q=c, A=A, l=l, u=u, verbose=False, max_iter=2000, eps_abs=1e-3, eps_rel=1e-3)\n\n    res = prob.solve()\n\n    # Check solution\n    if res.info.status != 'solved':\n        print(f\"Warning: OSQP status = {res.info.status}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_sol = res.x\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[idx_pg]\n    R_sol = x_sol[idx_r]\n    theta_sol = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta_sol[non_slack_indices] = x_sol[idx_theta]\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_c_lin * Pg_sol + 0.5 * gen_c_quad * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': res.info.status,\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'bus_to_theta_idx': bus_to_theta_idx,\n        'gen_c_lin': gen_c_lin,\n        'gen_c_quad': gen_c_quad\n    }\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef compute_lmp(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Compute LMP from marginal generation costs\"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    gen_data = results['gen_data']\n    Pg_sol = results['Pg']\n    gen_c_lin = results['gen_c_lin']\n    gen_c_quad = results['gen_c_quad']\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = bus_data[bus_idx, 0]\n        mc_list = []\n\n        for g_idx in range(len(gen_data)):\n            if gen_bus_ids[g_idx] == bus_id and gen_data[g_idx, 7] > 0:\n                # Marginal cost = c_lin + c_quad * Pg\n                mc = gen_c_lin[g_idx] + gen_c_quad[g_idx] * Pg_sol[g_idx]\n                mc_list.append(mc)\n\n        if mc_list:\n            lmp[bus_idx] = np.mean(mc_list)\n\n    return lmp\n\ndef run_analysis():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete analysis\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"Solving base case...\")\n    base_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    print(\"Solving counterfactual scenario (bus 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network, modify_line_capacity=(64, 1501, 1.2))\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Variable indices\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_theta)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_theta\n\n    # Cost vector: c = [c_lin_gen; 2*ones_reserve; 0_theta]\n    c = np.concatenate([gen_c_lin, np.full(n_gen, 2.0), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    h_diag = np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_gen), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n    P = diags(h_diag, format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    # 6. Line flow limits: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n\n    A_list = []\n    l_list = []\n    u_list = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraints (equality, so l = u)\n    # Build generator injection matrix G (non-slack buses x generators)\n    G = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_idx = bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx]\n            G[theta_idx, g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    G = G.tocsr()\n\n    # Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = pd_reduced\n    pd_reduced = pd[non_slack_indices]\n\n    A_pb = hstack([G, csr_matrix((n_theta, n_gen)), -B])\n    l_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n    u_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_pb)\n    l_list.append(l_pb)\n    u_list.append(u_pb)\n\n    # 2. Generator bounds (simple bounds, handled separately)\n    # Already handled through l_x, u_x\n\n    # 3. Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # Build as: [I I 0] * [Pg; R; theta] <= Pg_max\n    A_coup = hstack([eye(n_gen), eye(n_gen), csr_matrix((n_gen, n_theta))])\n    l_coup = np.full(n_gen, -np.inf)\n    u_coup = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_coup)\n    l_list.append(l_coup)\n    u_list.append(u_coup)\n\n    # 4. Reserve requirement constraint: -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n    # Or: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50.0)\n    A_res_req = hstack([csr_matrix((1, n_gen)), -np.ones((1, n_gen)), csr_matrix((1, n_theta))])\n    l_res_req = np.array([-np.inf])\n    u_res_req = np.array([-reserve_req])\n\n    A_list.append(A_res_req)\n    l_list.append(l_res_req)\n    u_list.append(u_res_req)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build row for this line: [0...0 b*(theta_from - theta_to) 0...0]\n        row = np.zeros(n_theta)\n\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n        else:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n\n        A_line = hstack([csr_matrix((1, 2 * n_gen)), csr_matrix(row).reshape(1, -1)])\n        A_line_list.append(A_line)\n        l_line_list.append(np.array([-rate]))\n        u_line_list.append(np.array([rate]))\n\n    if A_line_list:\n        A_lines = vstack(A_line_list)\n        l_lines = np.concatenate(l_line_list)\n        u_lines = np.concatenate(u_line_list)\n\n        A_list.append(A_lines)\n        l_list.append(l_lines)\n        u_list.append(u_lines)\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A = vstack(A_list).tocsr()\n    l = np.concatenate(l_list)\n    u = np.concatenate(u_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lb_x = np.full(n_var, -np.inf)\n    ub_x = np.full(n_var, np.inf)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[i] = gen_pmin[i]\n            ub_x[i] = gen_pmax[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[i] = 0\n            ub_x[i] = 0\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n\n    # Angle bounds\n    lb_x[2 * n_gen:] = -np.pi\n    ub_x[2 * n_gen:] = np.pi\n\n    # Create and solve problem with OSQP\n    prob = osqp.OSQP()\n    prob.setup(P=P, q=c, A=A, l=l, u=u, verbose=False, max_iter=2000, eps_abs=1e-3, eps_rel=1e-3)\n\n    res = prob.solve()\n\n    # Check solution\n    if res.info.status != 'solved':\n        print(f\"Warning: OSQP status = {res.info.status}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_sol = res.x\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[idx_pg]\n    R_sol = x_sol[idx_r]\n    theta_sol = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta_sol[non_slack_indices] = x_sol[idx_theta]\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_c_lin * Pg_sol + 0.5 * gen_c_quad * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': res.info.status,\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'bus_to_theta_idx': bus_to_theta_idx,\n        'gen_c_lin': gen_c_lin,\n        'gen_c_quad': gen_c_quad\n    }\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef compute_lmp(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Compute LMP from marginal generation costs\"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    gen_data = results['gen_data']\n    Pg_sol = results['Pg']\n    gen_c_lin = results['gen_c_lin']\n    gen_c_quad = results['gen_c_quad']\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = bus_data[bus_idx, 0]\n        mc_list = []\n\n        for g_idx in range(len(gen_data)):\n            if gen_bus_ids[g_idx] == bus_id and gen_data[g_idx, 7] > 0:\n                # Marginal cost = c_lin + c_quad * Pg\n                mc = gen_c_lin[g_idx] + gen_c_quad[g_idx] * Pg_sol[g_idx]\n                mc_list.append(mc)\n\n        if mc_list:\n            lmp[bus_idx] = np.mean(mc_list)\n\n    return lmp\n\ndef run_analysis():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete analysis\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"Solving base case...\")\n    base_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    print(\"Solving counterfactual scenario (bus 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_results = build_dc_opf_osqp(network, modify_line_capacity=(64, 1501, 1.2))\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_results['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_results['total_cost']:.2f}\")\n\n    #
