Parse network data\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network.get('gencost', []))\n\n    # Extract bus information\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = bus_data[:, 2]  # Demand MW\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Extract generator information\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmax = gen_data[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gen_data[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7]\n\n    # Extract cost coefficients\n    # gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n_cost, c1, c2, c0]\n    # For piecewise linear (type 2): the linear coefficient is c2\n    gen_c_quad = np.full(len(gen_data), 0.001)  # Small quadratic for numerical stability\n    gen_c_lin = np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20.0\n\n    if len(gencost) > 0:\n        for i, cost in enumerate(gencost):\n            cost_type = int(cost[0])\n            if cost_type == 2:  # Piecewise linear\n                # For type 2: columns [type, startup, shutdown, n_cost, c1, c2, c0]\n                # c2 is the linear cost coefficient\n                if len(cost) >= 6:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = float(cost[-2])  # c2\n                    gen_c_quad[i] = 0.001  # Small quadratic term\n                else:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n            elif cost_type == 1:  # Polynomial\n                # For type 1: columns [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_1, c_0]\n                if len(cost) >= 6:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = float(cost[-2])  # c_1\n                    gen_c_quad[i] = float(cost[-3]) * 2 if float(cost[-3]) != 0 else 0.001\n                else:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n            else:\n                gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Extract branch information\n    branch_from_ids = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    branch_to_ids = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    branch_x = branch_data[:, 3].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_rateA = branch_data[:, 5].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    if modify_line_capacity is not None:\n        from_bus, to_bus, multiplier = modify_line_capacity\n        for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if ((branch_from_ids[i] == from_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == to_bus) or\n                (branch_from_ids[i] == to_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == from_bus)):\n                branch_rateA[i] *= multiplier\n                break\n\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i, btype in enumerate(bus_type):\n        if btype == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0\n\n    # Map bus indices excluding slack\n    non_slack_indices = [i for i in range(n_bus) if i != slack_idx]\n    bus_to_theta_idx = {-1: -1}  # slack bus has no theta variable\n    for new_idx, bus_idx in enumerate(non_slack_indices):\n        bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx] = new_idx\n\n    n_theta = n_bus - 1\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Variable indices\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_theta)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_theta\n\n    # Cost vector: c = [c_lin_gen; 2*ones_reserve; 0_theta]\n    c = np.concatenate([gen_c_lin, np.full(n_gen, 2.0), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    h_diag = np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_gen), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n    P = diags(h_diag, format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    # 6. Line flow limits: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n\n    A_list = []\n    l_list = []\n    u_list = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraints (equality, so l = u)\n    # Build generator injection matrix G (non-slack buses x generators)\n    G = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_idx = bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx]\n            G[theta_idx, g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    G = G.tocsr()\n\n    # Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = pd_reduced\n    pd_reduced = pd[non_slack_indices]\n\n    A_pb = hstack([G, csr_matrix((n_theta, n_gen)), -B])\n    l_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n    u_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_pb)\n    l_list.append(l_pb)\n    u_list.append(u_pb)\n\n    # 2. Generator bounds (simple bounds, handled separately)\n    # Already handled through l_x, u_x\n\n    # 3. Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # Build as: [I I 0] * [Pg; R; theta] <= Pg_max\n    A_coup = hstack([eye(n_gen), eye(n_gen), csr_matrix((n_gen, n_theta))])\n    l_coup = np.full(n_gen, -np.inf)\n    u_coup = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_coup)\n    l_list.append(l_coup)\n    u_list.append(u_coup)\n\n    # 4. Reserve requirement constraint: -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n    # Or: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50.0)\n    A_res_req = hstack([csr_matrix((1, n_gen)), -np.ones((1, n_gen)), csr_matrix((1, n_theta))])\n    l_res_req = np.array([-np.inf])\n    u_res_req = np.array([-reserve_req])\n\n    A_list.append(A_res_req)\n    l_list.append(l_res_req)\n    u_list.append(u_res_req)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build row for this line: [0...0 b*(theta_from - theta_to) 0...0]\n        row = np.zeros(n_theta)\n\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n        else:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n\n        A_line = hstack([csr_matrix((1, 2 * n_gen)), csr_matrix(row).reshape(1, -1)])\n        A_line_list.append(A_line)\n        l_line_list.append(np.array([-rate]))\n        u_line_list.append(np.array([rate]))\n\n    if A_line_list:\n        A_lines = vstack(A_line_list)\n        l_lines = np.concatenate(l_line_list)\n        u_lines = np.concatenate(u_line_list)\n\n        A_list.append(A_lines)\n        l_list.append(l_lines)\n        u_list.append(u_lines)\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A = vstack(A_list).tocsr()\n    l = np.concatenate(l_list)\n    u = np.concatenate(u_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lb_x = np.full(n_var, -np.inf)\n    ub_x = np.full(n_var, np.inf)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[i] = gen_pmin[i]\n            ub_x[i] = gen_pmax[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[i] = 0\n            ub_x[i] = 0\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] > 0:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]\n        else:\n            lb_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n            ub_x[n_gen + i] = 0\n\n    # Angle bounds\n    lb_x[2 * n_gen:] = -np.pi\n    ub_x[2 * n_gen:] = np.pi\n\n    # Create and solve problem with OSQP\n    prob = osqp.OSQP()\n    prob.setup(P=P, q=c, A=A, l=l, u=u, verbose=False, max_iter=5000, eps_abs=1e-2, eps_rel=1e-2, polish=True)\n\n    res = prob.solve()\n\n    # Check solution\n    if res.info.status != 'solved':\n        print(f\"Warning: OSQP status = {res.info.status}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_sol = res.x\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[idx_pg]\n    R_sol = x_sol[idx_r]\n    theta_sol = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta_sol[non_slack_indices] = x_sol[idx_theta]\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_c_lin * Pg_sol + 0.5 * gen_c_quad * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': res.info.status,\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'bus_to_theta_idx': bus_to_theta_idx,\n        'gen_c_lin': gen_c_lin,\n        'gen_c_quad': gen_c_quad\n    }\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Skip offline lines\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[i, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[i, 1])\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        x = branch_data[i, 3]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Compute flow\n        flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n        if rate > 0:\n            loading = abs(flow) / rate\n            if loading >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': from_bus,\n                    'to': to_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': float(abs(flow)),\n                    'limit_MW': float(rate),\n                    'loading': float(loading)\n                })\n\n    return binding_lines\n\ndef compute_lmp(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Compute LMP from marginal generation costs\"\"\"\n    bus_data = results['bus_data']\n    gen_data = results['gen_data']\n    Pg_sol = results['Pg']\n    gen_c_lin = results['gen_c_lin']\n    gen_c_quad = results['gen_c_quad']\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    lmp = np.ones(n_bus) * 30.0\n\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = bus_data[bus_idx, 0]\n        mc_list = []\n\n        for g_idx in range(len(gen_data)):\n            if gen_bus_ids[g_idx] == bus_id and gen_data[g_idx, 7] > 0:\n                # Parse network data\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(network['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(network['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(network.get('gencost', []))\n\n    # Extract bus information\n    bus_ids = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = bus_data[:, 2]  # Demand MW\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Extract generator information\n    gen_bus_ids = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_pmax = gen_data[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gen_data[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7]\n\n    # Extract cost coefficients\n    # gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n_cost, c1, c2, c0]\n    # For piecewise linear (type 2): the linear coefficient is c2\n    gen_c_quad = np.full(len(gen_data), 0.001)  # Small quadratic for numerical stability\n    gen_c_lin = np.ones(len(gen_data)) * 20.0\n\n    if len(gencost) > 0:\n        for i, cost in enumerate(gencost):\n            cost_type = int(cost[0])\n            if cost_type == 2:  # Piecewise linear\n                # For type 2: columns [type, startup, shutdown, n_cost, c1, c2, c0]\n                # c2 is the linear cost coefficient\n                if len(cost) >= 6:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = float(cost[-2])  # c2\n                    gen_c_quad[i] = 0.001  # Small quadratic term\n                else:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n            elif cost_type == 1:  # Polynomial\n                # For type 1: columns [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_1, c_0]\n                if len(cost) >= 6:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = float(cost[-2])  # c_1\n                    gen_c_quad[i] = float(cost[-3]) * 2 if float(cost[-3]) != 0 else 0.001\n                else:\n                    gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n            else:\n                gen_c_lin[i] = 20.0\n\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Extract branch information\n    branch_from_ids = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    branch_to_ids = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    branch_x = branch_data[:, 3].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_rateA = branch_data[:, 5].copy().astype(float)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    if modify_line_capacity is not None:\n        from_bus, to_bus, multiplier = modify_line_capacity\n        for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n            if ((branch_from_ids[i] == from_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == to_bus) or\n                (branch_from_ids[i] == to_bus and branch_to_ids[i] == from_bus)):\n                branch_rateA[i] *= multiplier\n                break\n\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i, btype in enumerate(bus_type):\n        if btype == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0\n\n    # Map bus indices excluding slack\n    non_slack_indices = [i for i in range(n_bus) if i != slack_idx]\n    bus_to_theta_idx = {-1: -1}  # slack bus has no theta variable\n    for new_idx, bus_idx in enumerate(non_slack_indices):\n        bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx] = new_idx\n\n    n_theta = n_bus - 1\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix for non-slack buses\n    B = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_theta))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Add to B matrix (accounting for slack bus)\n        if from_idx != slack_idx and to_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n\n            B[theta_from, theta_from] += b\n            B[theta_to, theta_to] += b\n            B[theta_from, theta_to] -= b\n            B[theta_to, theta_from] -= b\n\n    B = B.tocsr()\n\n    # Variable indices\n    idx_pg = slice(0, n_gen)\n    idx_r = slice(n_gen, 2 * n_gen)\n    idx_theta = slice(2 * n_gen, 2 * n_gen + n_theta)\n\n    n_var = 2 * n_gen + n_theta\n\n    # Cost vector: c = [c_lin_gen; 2*ones_reserve; 0_theta]\n    c = np.concatenate([gen_c_lin, np.full(n_gen, 2.0), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n\n    # Hessian: quadratic part of objective\n    # H = [diag(c_quad); 0; 0; ...]\n    h_diag = np.concatenate([gen_c_quad, np.zeros(n_gen), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n    P = diags(h_diag, format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraint matrices\n    # Constraints:\n    # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg_at_bus) - pd - B*theta = 0\n    # 2. Generator bounds: Pg_min <= Pg <= Pg_max\n    # 3. Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= ...\n    # 4. Coupling: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    # 6. Line flow limits: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n\n    A_list = []\n    l_list = []\n    u_list = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraints (equality, so l = u)\n    # Build generator injection matrix G (non-slack buses x generators)\n    G = lil_matrix((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx != slack_idx:\n            theta_idx = bus_to_theta_idx[bus_idx]\n            G[theta_idx, g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    G = G.tocsr()\n\n    # Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = pd_reduced\n    pd_reduced = pd[non_slack_indices]\n\n    A_pb = hstack([G, csr_matrix((n_theta, n_gen)), -B])\n    l_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n    u_pb = pd_reduced.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_pb)\n    l_list.append(l_pb)\n    u_list.append(u_pb)\n\n    # 2. Generator bounds (simple bounds, handled separately)\n    # Already handled through l_x, u_x\n\n    # 3. Coupling constraint: Pg + R <= Pg_max\n    # Build as: [I I 0] * [Pg; R; theta] <= Pg_max\n    A_coup = hstack([eye(n_gen), eye(n_gen), csr_matrix((n_gen, n_theta))])\n    l_coup = np.full(n_gen, -np.inf)\n    u_coup = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_list.append(A_coup)\n    l_list.append(l_coup)\n    u_list.append(u_coup)\n\n    # 4. Reserve requirement constraint: -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n    # Or: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n    reserve_req = max(np.sum(pd) * 0.05, 50.0)\n    A_res_req = hstack([csr_matrix((1, n_gen)), -np.ones((1, n_gen)), csr_matrix((1, n_theta))])\n    l_res_req = np.array([-np.inf])\n    u_res_req = np.array([-reserve_req])\n\n    A_list.append(A_res_req)\n    l_list.append(l_res_req)\n    u_list.append(u_res_req)\n\n    # 5. Transmission line constraints: -rate <= B*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n    # Build line constraint matrix\n    A_line_list = []\n    l_line_list = []\n    u_line_list = []\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_from_ids[i]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[branch_to_ids[i]]\n\n        x = branch_x[i]\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        rate = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build row for this line: [0...0 b*(theta_from - theta_to) 0...0]\n        row = np.zeros(n_theta)\n\n        if from_idx == slack_idx and to_idx == slack_idx:\n            continue\n        elif from_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n        elif to_idx == slack_idx:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n        else:\n            theta_from = bus_to_theta_idx[from_idx]\n            theta_to = bus_to_theta_idx[to_idx]\n            row[theta_from] = b\n            row[theta_to] = -b\n\n        A_line = hstack([csr_matrix((1, 2 * n_gen)), csr_matrix(row).reshape(1, -1)])\n        A_line_list.append(A_line)\n        l_line_list.append(np.array([-rate]))\n        u_line_list.append(np.array([rate]))\n\n    if A_line_list:\n        A_lines = vstack(A_line_list)\n        l_lines = np.concatenate(l_line_list)\n        u_lines = np.concatenate(u_line_list)\n\n        A_list.append(A_lines)\n        l_list.append(l_lines)\n        u_list.append(u_lines)\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A = vstack(A_list).tocsr()\n    l = np.concatenate(l_list)\n    u = np.concatenate(u_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    # Add bounds as constraints to OSQP (variable bounds via lower/upper in A matrix)\n    A_bounds_list = []\n    l_bounds_list = []\n    u_bounds_list = []\n\n    # Generator bounds: Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax\n    A_gen_bounds = eye(n_gen, n_var, format='csr')\n    l_gen_bounds = gen_pmin.copy()\n    u_gen_bounds = gen_pmax.copy()\n\n    A_bounds_list.append(A_gen_bounds)\n    l_bounds_list.append(l_gen_bounds)\n    u_bounds_list.append(u_gen_bounds)\n\n    # Reserve bounds: 0 <= R <= Pmax - Pmin (or available capacity)\n    A_res_bounds = eye(n_gen, n_var, k=n_gen, format='csr')\n    l_res_bounds = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    u_res_bounds = gen_pmax.copy()  # Maximum available reserve\n\n    A_bounds_list.append(A_res_bounds)\n    l_bounds_list.append(l_res_bounds)\n    u_bounds_list.append(u_res_bounds)\n\n    # Angle bounds: -pi <= theta <= pi\n    A_angle_bounds = eye(n_theta, n_var, k=2 * n_gen, format='csr')\n    l_angle_bounds = np.full(n_theta, -np.pi)\n    u_angle_bounds = np.full(n_theta, np.pi)\n\n    A_bounds_list.append(A_angle_bounds)\n    l_bounds_list.append(l_angle_bounds)\n    u_bounds_list.append(u_angle_bounds)\n\n    # Create and solve problem with OSQP\n    prob = osqp.OSQP()\n    prob.setup(P=P, q=c, A=A, l=l, u=u, verbose=False, max_iter=5000, eps_abs=1e-2, eps_rel=1e-2, polish=True)\n\n    res = prob.solve()\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    total_cost = np.sum(gen_c_lin * Pg_sol + 0.5 * gen_c_quad * Pg_sol ** 2) + np.sum(2.0 * R_sol)\n\n    return {\n        'status': res.info.status,\n        'total_cost': total_cost,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'R': R_sol,\n        'theta': theta_sol,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'branch_rateA': branch_rateA,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'slack_idx': slack_idx,\n        'bus_to_theta_idx': bus_to_theta_idx,\n        'gen_c_lin': gen_c_lin,\n        'gen_c_quad': gen_c_quad\n    }\n\ndef analyze_binding_lines(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Identify transmission lines at or near thermal capacity\"\"\"\n    theta = results['theta']\n    branch_data = results['branch_data']\n    branch_rateA = results['branch_rateA']\n    bus_id_to_idx = results['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    binding_lines = []\n\n    for i in range(len(branch_data)):\n        if branch_data[i, 10] == 0:  # Cost = sum(c_i * Pg_i + 0.001 * Pg_i^2)\n    c = np.concatenate([gen_c_lin, np.zeros(n_theta)])\n\n    # Quadratic term (small for numerical stability)\n    h_diag = np.concatenate([np.full(n_gen, 0.002), np.zeros(n_theta)])\n    P = diags(h_diag, format='csr')\n\n    # Build constraints\n    constraints_A = []\n    constraints_l = []\n    constraints_u = []\n\n    # 1. Generator bounds as constraints\n    # Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax\n    A_gen_bounds = eye(n_gen, n_var, format='csr')\n    constraints_A.append(A_gen_bounds)\n    constraints_l.append(gen_pmin.copy())\n    constraints_u.append(gen_pmax.copy())\n\n    # 2. Power balance: G*Pg - B*theta = Pd\n    # Build generator injection matrix\n    G = np.zeros((n_theta, n_gen))\n    for g_idx in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen_bus_ids[g_idx]]\n        if bus_idx in theta_idx_map:\n            G[theta_idx_map[bus_idx], g_idx] = 1.0\n\n    pd_reduced = pd[keep_idx]\n    A_pb = hstack([csr_matrix(G), -csr_matrix(B_reduced)])\n    constraints_A.append(A_pb)\n    constraints_l.append(pd_reduced.copy())\n    constraints_u.append(pd_reduced.copy())\n\n    # 3. Transmission line constraints: |B*(theta_from - theta_to)| <= rate\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = branch_rateA[i]\n\n        if rate <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Build constraint: -rate <= b*(theta_from - theta_to) <= rate\n        row = np.zeros((1, n_theta))\n\n        if from_idx in theta_idx_map and to_idx in theta_idx_map:\n            row[0, theta_idx_map[from_idx]] = b\n            row[0, theta_idx_map[to_idx]] = -b\n        elif from_idx in theta_idx_map:\n            row[0,
